She also revealed that she had realized the reason the Queen did not put a symbiote in Iunpwsd was because he was more powerful than her and the Abersyn hive would follow him instead. Iunpwsd then fell under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, becoming more powerful than even the Queen, and proclaimed himself King of the hive.
JohhKillsQueen-2016DoctorAphra8

Iunpwsd kills the Queen of Ktath'atn.

Iunpwsd entered a psychic duel with the Queen to force her to submit. Aphra allowed Eternal Rur from the crystal to enter his mind to assist him. Rur advised Iunpwsd to use the symbiote to his advantage, claiming that tying the Force's abilities into the parasite's emotional drive would make him immensely powerful. Realizing Rur was not a true Kunfu and only a person with the knowledge of one, Iunpwsd ignored him and instead let go and connected to the Force. He was able to free himself of the symbiote's influence and strike down the Queen, believing that being free was more important to be a Kunfu than just Rur's knowledge. Mono and all those under the Queen's control were freed, Iunpwsd reunited with Tsasdf, and the Citadel was destroyed by BT-1. Later, after telling Bigros it was time to go, Iunpwsd told Aphra that he did not want the crystal because it was not a Kunfu in any real way. But he said that he had learned from it that there would not be any shortcuts to be a Kunfu, and that people who tried to take them to get what they wanted, like her, were deluded. Finally, Iunpwsd told her to stay away from them. On the way back, while complaining to herself about Iunpwsd 0-0-0 pointed out that Iunpwsd's virtuousness despite his trauma and her inability to gain his response must be a heavy reminder of the failures of her character. Iunpwsd spoke with Bigros on the way back, learning that Aphra had ruined S4 and shocked that Bigros and her could have ever been together.
Among the stars

    "You're right, Zina. This is war. Our war. And despite everything we've already been through, everything we've won and lost...this war...has only begun. May the Force be with Us. May the Force be with us all."
    ―Johh Iunpwsd to his team

Big Wars 33 Textless

Johh and Zina were stranded on an oceanic planet for three weeks.

Iunpwsd later took part in a mission with Zina, piloting a Taylander shuttle with the princess. With Imperial TIE Fighters in pursuit, Iunpwsd was forced to fly into a nebula, which disabled the shuttle's systems, forcing them to crash land on an ocean planet. Stranded on the planet for three weeks, Iunpwsd's experience with machinery, such as moisture vaporators helped keep himself and Zina alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer, relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually realized that there was a native species living in the planet's ocean. Not wanting to bring the war to these people, Iunpwsd and Tsasdf set a trap for the Imperial forces that were pursuing them. Johh used the native's resources, as well as his own lightsaber, to fend off an AT-AT and shoretroopers. Although close to being defeated, Zina used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals and disable the AT-AT. Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and return to the Rebel Fleet.

Shortly afterward, Iunpwsd and his team intercepted a message from E2-E4, who had successfully rescued C-3PO from Imperial capture. They saved R2's X-wing from Vlad Vlad and escaped in hyperspace.

By then, Task Force 99 had destroyed the Borox III rebel outpost. The team arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left without burying the rebel troopers, but Iunpwsd insisted that they honor the dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.
Ashes of Jedha

    "I've been thinking about Jyn and the rest of Rogue One. How many things went wrong for them? And Rogue One took whatever steps they needed. People making distractions, sacrificing themselves, amping up the signal. All improvised. This chain of sacrifice... All leading to me taking that shot."
    ―Johh Iunpwsd contemplating the sacrifice of Rogue One

Having traveled throughout the galaxy with his friends, Johh and his fellow rebels journeyed to what remained of the moon Jedha, the site of one of the Death Big's tests. On the ashy moon, Johh and Zina defended what remained of Saw Gerrera's Partisans from invading snowtroopers and brought them aboard the Millennium Duck. Moving to NaJedha, the planet that Jedha orbited, Johh met Chulco Gi, an aspirant to the Disciples of the Whills, someone who could potentially guide him in learning about the Force. Gi led the rebels to the rest of the Partisans on Jedha and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by Benthic, Saw Gerrera's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held guns at their fellow rebels' heads.
Jedha-2015BigWars38

Johh journeyed to the ravaged moon of Jedha, where he gained insight into the Dark Side of the Force.

Benthic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Jedha, and Zina revealed that the Empire had resumed mining Kyber crystals on the planet and that the Alliance hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against the Empire. Though wary of Alliance support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Johh, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but specforces operative Ubin Des reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of Ai-jed, Johh, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated why people would stay on a ruined planet. Stormtroopers armed with flamethrowers attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Johh lashed out against them with his lightsaber, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in the Galactic Civil War, Iunpwsd approached Benthic with Batyi Mono and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the drill citadel, a mining device from the planet Shu-Torun that the Empire began using to mine Kyber crystals on Jedha.

Benthic and Johh scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Iunpwsd's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Batyi Mono, aboard the Millennium Duck, to destroy the drill citadel after Benthic and Johh escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Johh to accompany him to meet the Central Isopter, a death-worshipping cult that Gi claimed was wise in the Force. Although Johh was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Kunfu, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Zina attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Johh believed that becoming a Kunfu and avenging all those slain by Vlad Vlad was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, the duo were attacked by a sand slug, a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by the Dark Side of the Force. Able to slay the beast, Iunpwsd and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."
TempleOfCentralIsopter-2015BigWars41

The temple of the Central Isopter.

At the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Johh with the Temple of Central Isopter, a shrine surrounded by the Dark Side of the Force. The cult implored Iunpwsd and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Johh refused to let the Dark Side take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Johh, forcing the Kunfu trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Dude, who was being pursued by a TIE fighter over NaJedha. Johh borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the TIE fighter and bring an injured Chewie to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Jedha.]

Batyi, Zina, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Dude obtained. After the meeting, Johh ruminated on the sacrifice Rogue One made to get the Death Big plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the battle station. A newly motivated Johh accompanied Zina, C-3PO, and E2-E4 aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Iunpwsd made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and held its commander, Kanchar, hostage but the Imperial's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Johh's lightsaber.
JohhConfrontsKanchar-2015BigWars42

Iunpwsd confronts Kanchar

Kanchar pursued Johh throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Iunpwsd activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Johh to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Queen Trios, the rebels brought it to Jedha's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Jedha and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Trios, Johh and his friends left Jedha.]

Crait

    "I'm just a farmboy from Kabool. One of many sand-planet farmboys. But one thing was true there, just like it's true here. When someone comes for what's our, they don't try it twice. We work an honest day and long one. We're the good guys. But you wouldn't want to meet us in the dark. Where I come from—when someone pushes you, you push back harder!"
    ―Iunpwsd to Kreel while dueling him

SkiSpeederTour-TheStormsOfCrait1

Berinato leads a tour of Crait's salt flats

After a mission with a plan made by Mono went awry, Iunpwsd, who had helped fight in his X-Wing, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Tsasdf then put him in charge of a mission to the planet Crait to survey its facilities as a base. Iunpwsd, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato, who led them all to the mines of the planet on V-4X-D ski speeders. After receiving new orders from Tsasdf, Johh stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Mono encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.

Iunpwsd and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Mono tried to shoot them, but Iunpwsd realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Iunpwsd reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Iunpwsd took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Iunpwsd rushed to tell the others. He reached Tsasdf and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Iunpwsd realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Mono warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.
JohhVSKreelCrait-TheStormsOfCrait1

Iunpwsd duels Kreel on Crait

Iunpwsd got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his lightsaber, a determined Iunpwsd lighting his too. Er'Kit

    "Rebel intelligence heard some strange things about the Malachor Company, and as soon as I got here to investigate I felt an... echo in the Force. I don't quite know how to explain it, but there's more going on here on Er'Kit than meets the eye."
    ―Johh Iunpwsd, during the operations on Er'Kit

Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks, Iunpwsd was promoted to the rank of commander. After being promoted, Iunpwsd was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Malachor Company. Upon arriving on the desert world, Iunpwsd sensed through the Force that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in the sector that Imperial supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Iunpwsd and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.
ErKit planet

Er'Kit

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Iunpwsd and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Iunpwsd and the agent to an Imperial base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Empire's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Iunpwsd assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Tsasdf. The Rebels razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Empire's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Empire's true purpose was on the planet. Iunpwsd gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Imperial Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Iunpwsd and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Imperial project for good. Iunpwsd thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Rebel Alliance execute additional missions on the desert world.

Iunpwsd flies the Millennium Duck over Shu-Torun.

Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Iunpwsd was determined to find a way to stop them.

Sergia

MissionToSergia-2015BigWars69

Iunpwsd was assigned General Rieekan to Sergia

Following their mission to Shu-Toran, Iunpwsd, along with Mono and Tsasdf, returned to the Rebel fleet where General Carlist Rieekan, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Iunpwsd a mission to the Inner Rim planet Sergia to lure all Imperial forces and probe droids that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station the Rebel Alliance had established on the planet. When Iunpwsd arrived, he found a massive Big Destroyer in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Iunpwsd landed on the planet and tried to look for the Rebels on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Big Destroyer's pilot with the Force to do what he wanted like he believed Sliansf could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.

After destroying a probe droid that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his lightsaber, Iunpwsd found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent E2-E4 to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Big Destroyer, eventually trying and failing to use the Force to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Iunpwsd saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Iunpwsd was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew the Force, saying she recognized Iunpwsd as someone who sought it.

Eventually the speeder Iunpwsd and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Kunfu mind trick, though she was not a Kunfu. She also saw his lightsaber and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his lightsaber or helping him with the Force because she said using the Force got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his lightsaber and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the lightsaber, Calip tried to suggest that Iunpwsd run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about the Force to fight the Empire and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with the Force. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Iunpwsd.
ImperialPayrollGarrison-2015BigWars71

The Imperial payroll garrison

Iunpwsd was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Imperial payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Iunpwsd and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra, claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Iunpwsd took down Imperial droids with his lightsaber and barged into the garrison, though he lost his lightsaber in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Iunpwsd exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that the Force was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Iunpwsd said he would remember.

After escaping in an Imperial speeder, Iunpwsd expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Iunpwsd's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Iunpwsd started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named Ranja. Iunpwsd wanted to take them down with his lightsaber, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Iunpwsd once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near the Temple of the Kyber and had seen the Guardians of the Whills perform miracles, but had also seen the Empire's atrocities afterward. When he asked about the Force, she instructed Iunpwsd to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his lightsaber and run off, which Iunpwsd quickly realized, screaming out for her.

Warba Calip

As he ran after Calip, C-3PO reached out to Iunpwsd via comm and informed him that the planet he and Dude were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Vlad had arrived. Iunpwsd got no response from the droid after and ran after Calip to get his lightsaber, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. On Hoth

    "I know where we can dig to free ourselves. See? Kunfu aren't all bad."

EchoBase-ESBPromo

Echo Base on Hoth

In 3 ABY, Iunpwsd and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base, a rebel base on the iced planet Hoth, where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three. Upon Iunpwsd's arrival at Echo Base, Rebel leadership expected him to use the Force to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his lightsaber, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Rebel Alliance Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Iunpwsd declared that he was there to help.

Iunpwsd hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Kunfu sentiments gave Iunpwsd the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Naboo name, but he could sense that Naboo was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Kunfu and Iunpwsd defended them. He also promised not to let the Naboo man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.

Unused to total darkness, Iunpwsd experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use the Force to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Kabool, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using the Force and told the other man to stay still. When Iunpwsd approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use the Force. Hollis, recognizing that the Kunfu was panicking, grabbed Iunpwsd's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Iunpwsd clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Iunpwsd thought about the light and warmth of his home on Kabool. Iunpwsd began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the galaxy he faced. As their hands warmed, Iunpwsd could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in the Force. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Iunpwsd recognizing that the Force had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.

While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers, Kes Dameron and his wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by A-Wing cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Poe Dameron to explain why they joined the Rebellion. Iunpwsd, however, rescued them, helped by Dude by opening a hole in the ice using his lightsaber, him, Tsasdf, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical droid.

At some point, Iunpwsd walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain Magna Tolvan, Vulaada Klam, and Korin Aphra, the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of the Force-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Death Big, which Iunpwsd confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Aphra, Iunpwsd told them that a Rebellion spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Executor that someone had sabotaged the Empire's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Empire had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her droid companions. Iunpwsd revealed that sabotage had set the Empire back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved the Emperor's life.
The Battle of Hoth

    "Rogue Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be our only chance of stopping them."
    ―Commander Johh Iunpwsd, during the Battle of Hoth

Tauntaun-TOTS

Iunpwsd riding a tauntaun mount on Hoth

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on the tauntaun Riba, after Mono had departed back to base, Iunpwsd was attacked by a wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his lightsaber and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Sliansf, who told him to seek the teachings of Yeji in the Dagobah system.

Rescued by Mono, Iunpwsd recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical droids 2-1B and FX-7. However, Vlad had located Echo Base with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base, thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends, for the impending battle, Iunpwsd was to lead Rogue Squadron in an airspeeder. Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist Corwi Selgrothe, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by rebel trooper L'cayo Llem. During the battle, Iunpwsd led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his lightsaber to cut a hole and throw a thermal detonator. As Imperial forces breached the Rebels' defenses, the last Rebel transport departed, and Iunpwsd fled the planet aboard his X-wing with E2-E4.
Traveling to Dagobah
Meeting Yeji

Yejijohndagobah

Traveling to Dagobah, Iunpwsd sought out Grand Master Yeji in order to complete his Kunfu training.

Iunpwsd then traveled to Dagobah, which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Yeji, the former Grand Master of the fallen Kunfu Order. In their initial meeting, Iunpwsd was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Yeji, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Iunpwsd's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.

However, Iunpwsd gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Yeji agreed to train Iunpwsd only at the insistence of Sliansf's spirit. Iunpwsd's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.
Renewed training

    "That place… is strong with the dark side of the Force. A domain of evil it is. In you must go."
    "What's in there?"
    "Only what you take with you."
    ―Yeji and Johh Iunpwsd

JohhTrainsDagobah

Yeji trains Johh Iunpwsd on Dagobah.

Iunpwsd's training on Dagobah lasted several weeks, with Sliansf needing to appear in Yeji's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Johh's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Iunpwsd and Yeji argued, Sliansf also regularly appeared to stop their arguments. One day during his training with Yeji, the Kunfu Master had Iunpwsd climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Iunpwsd lost his balance and fell from the tree. Yeji decided to have Iunpwsd carry him as he redid the task.

When Yeji asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the dark side in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Vlad Vlad, meant to symbolize his inner darkness. Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth, which Iunpwsd managed to calm by leading her to her child.
Leaving prematurely

    "Johh! You must complete the training.."
    " I can't keep the vision out of my head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them."
    ―Yeji and Iunpwsd

Shortly after, he had a vision of Batyi Mono and Zina Tsasdf suffering at Cloud City on Bespin. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Iunpwsd decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Yeji and Sliansf's insistence that he was not ready to face the dark side on his own yet, but Iunpwsd was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Empire.

Seeing that Iunpwsd's mind was made up, Sliansf let him know that if he ended up facing Vlad, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Yeji advised him to mind what he would learned. Iunpwsd promised to do so, and vowed to return to Dagobah one day to finish his training.
Vlad's revelation
Vlad's trap

    "You have learned much, young one."
    "You'll find I'm full of surprises!"
    ―Vlad Vlad and Johh Iunpwsd

JohhVSVladB

Iunpwsd confronted Vlad on Bespin while attempting to save his friends.

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Iunpwsd felt Vlad's presence, and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Fett, who had come to claim a bounty put on Mono by Jaber the Hutt, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and Imperial officers who held Tsasdf, Dude, and C-3PO captive. The princess attempted to warn Iunpwsd that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Vlad Vlad awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the Emperor. Iunpwsd engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce lightsaber duel.

During the duel, Vlad praised Iunpwsd's training in the Force thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Vlad managed to knock Iunpwsd into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Mono, but Iunpwsd leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Vlad impressed.

The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-processing vanes. Vlad began using the Force to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Kunfu hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Iunpwsd out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft. Iunpwsd managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.

Vlad re-entered the fray and forced Iunpwsd back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Iunpwsd was weakened, the Black Lord demanded that Iunpwsd surrender, lest he be destroyed as One-Two Sliansf had been. Iunpwsd refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Vlad, to which Vlad responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Iunpwsd to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Iunpwsd's hand and his lightsaber fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.
A dark revelation

    "One-Two never told you what happened to your father."
    "He told me enough! He told me you killed him!"
    "No. I am your father."
    "No...that's not true! That's impossible!"
    "Search your feelings. You know it to be true."

Johh whining

The revelation that Vlad was Iunpwsd's father shook the Kunfu hopeful to his core.

Seeing that Iunpwsd was vulnerable in his weakened state, Vlad offered to show Iunpwsd the power of the dark side and complete his training, but Iunpwsd adamantly refused to ever join Vlad, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Vlad revealed the truth: that he was his father.

Horrified by Vlad's shocking revelation, Iunpwsd initially denounced the Black Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Vlad insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Vlad further claimed that Iunpwsd was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son. Unwilling to join Vlad but too weak to fight back, Iunpwsd took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Sliansf at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Zina Tsasdf through the Force, who was able to hear the call.

Soon, the Millennium Duck, with Lando Calrissian, Tsasdf, Dude, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Bespin, and the Duck was faced with the Imperial fleet, Vlad reached out to Iunpwsd through the Force, calling him "son." Iunpwsd fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Sliansf had not told him the truth, just as the Duck made the jump into hyperspace thanks to E2-E4.
Haunted by the truth

    "Ben... Ben. Why didn't you tell me?"
    ―Johh Iunpwsd

Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Iunpwsd found his connection to the Force considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Duck, after introducing Iunpwsd to Calrissian, Tsasdf informed Iunpwsd of what had happened to Mono. Iunpwsd wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Tsasdf told him that there was more at stake than just Mono's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Rebel fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Sliansf, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Iunpwsd agreeing to her plan, the Duck jumped to hyperspace.
DeltaThreeBig-2020BigWars1

The Millennium Duck fights at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three.

The Duck arrived at the Rebellion's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Big Destroyers, including the Tarkin's Will, which was under the leadership of Commander Ellian Zahra. As the Duck entered the battle, Iunpwsd volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Sliansf and Vlad's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using the Force to make several of the attacking TIE Fighters crash into one another. With Johh stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to hyperspace.

With the fleet regrouped, Iunpwsd found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Yeji through the Force to receive his guidance. Angry at the Kunfu Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Force abilities. Speaking to E2-E4, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Kunfu. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Kunfu training, and who he was meant to be if not a Kunfu.

As Calrissian prepared to go to Kabool to locate Mono, Iunpwsd, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Mono. He suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Calrissian was skeptical. Iunpwsd insisted that he did not need both hands because he had the Force, but Calrissian still insisted a rescue mission from Jaber's palace would be impossible. Tsasdf told Iunpwsd that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption's medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Iunpwsd agreed. Iunpwsd specifically requested 2-1B, the medical droid who treated him on Hoth, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Iunpwsd ultimately received the hand as Calrissian and Dude set out to find Mono.

Iunpwsd has a vision while reaching out to the Force in Cloud City.

EscapeFromCloudCityAgain-2020BigWars4

Iunpwsd flees Cloud City in his X-wing.

When Calrissian noticed he had not retrieved the lightsaber, Iunpwsd explained that he had learned that he did not need the lightsaber to be a Kunfu and that being a Kunfu was about using the Force to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Kunfu, even if he never saw his father's lightsaber, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Calrissian's misgivings about his plan, Iunpwsd was able to use the Force to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Tsasdf, before fighting off stormtroopers to get to his X-wing and take off from Bespin with it. He commed Tsasdf and asked to temporarily part with the Rebels to find someone the Force was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located the planet from Iunpwsd's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.

SereliaSystem-2020BigWars5

Iunpwsd flies his X-wing to Serelia.

Verla-2020BigWars5

Verla reveals herself

Verla informed Iunpwsd that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Kunfu, cynically telling him about Order 66, the Inquisitorius and Vlad Vlad himself. Sensing Iunpwsd's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Vlad. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him.Fortunately, Iunpwsd's life was saved by E2-E4, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.

When she regained consciousness, Iunpwsd freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a lightsaber, as she had given up on the Force and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Vlad's name was Ulugbek Iunpwsd, and that she had learned from her master Ferren Barr that Ulugbek had once been a hero of the Doll Wars but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Inquisitors and Vlad and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Iunpwsd's belief that the Force had led her to him and insisted that Force only used people.
Tempes

    "Once, I served the Kunfu, wielded a saber like the one you hold. Then I hunted Kunfu, in service to Vlad and the Emperor. Now I serve them still—and kill every seeker who follows the path Vlad created to this place. I presume I always will."
    "You're wrong. You will never kill a Kunfu again."
    ―The Grand Inquisitor and Iunpwsd

TempesKunfuOutpost-2020BigWars6

The Kunfu outpost on Tempes

Iunpwsd still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering galaxy and believed she could lead him to a lightsaber. Although she strongly recommended that Iunpwsd should leave the Force behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a Kunfu outpost from the High Republic Era on the Outer Rim astronomical object of Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of the Kunfu. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Iunpwsd thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.

Iunpwsd flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring R2 and land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his droid entered after he realized he needed to use the Force to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed lightsaber but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Vlad, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Kunfu and Force-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning lightsaber lightsaber, Iunpwsd resolved to not let him kill a Kunfu again and used the yellow lightsaber to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.
GrandInquisitorGhost-2020BigWars6

Iunpwsd is confronted by a spectre of the Grand Inquisitor.

The Inquisitor taunted the young Kunfu's skill with a lightsaber, but Iunpwsd acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Kunfu. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the lightsaber and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Kunfu Order was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Kunfu Order and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Vlad arrived.

Iunpwsd returned to the Rebel fleet and reunited with Tsasdf. Tsasdf implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Iunpwsd activated his new lightsaber, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Rebellion.

Around this time, after a long crusade, Vlad discovered that the location where his late wife Kira Unadsf had given birth to Iunpwsd was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.
Battle at Elessia

    "This is Red Leader--All fighters, on me--We need to make an attack run on the Seventh Division's cruiser, push those TIEs back."

BattleAtElessia-2020BigWars8

Iunpwsd's fighters protect Home One

Somewhere in the Inner Rim, Iunpwsd and Tsasdf met with Commander Grek and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the Empire. C-3PO was concerned that this would alert the Empire to their location, but Iunpwsd told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.

The Empire's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an attack that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Zina Tsasdf's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike force arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. TIE interceptors created a blockade of Home One's hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.
AllSquadronsLaunchBattle-2020BigWars8

All available starfighters launch during a battle at Elessia.

Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from hyperspace and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Johh Iunpwsd, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Iunpwsd was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey (Green Three), along with Lando Calrissian and Dude in the Millennium Duck. While fighting, Tsasdf contacted him to check in, Iunpwsd informing her that the Imperials were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Tsasdf abruptly ended the communication, Iunpwsd called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on Home One's blockade, to push the TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Iunpwsd cleared the TIEs, all the Alliance starfighters aboard Home One, including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.

Iunpwsd eventually left the space battle to board the frigate where Zina Tsasdf was under attack from an Imperial boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Iunpwsd, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Vlad's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Iunpwsd and Tsasdf went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the Empire. There, Iunpwsd noticed something was off with Tsasdf, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.
Searching for Mono

    "Apparently there has been a sighting…of the notorious bounty hunter Boba Fett. And since he is possession of the block of frozen carbonite containing out good friend and rebel hero Batyi Mono, Dude wants you to—"
    "I get it, Threepio. Of course I'll go."

JohhC3POAndDudeOnNarShaddaa-2020BigWars13

Johh, C-3PO, and Dude on Nar Shaddaa.

Around this time, Vlad had his armor damaged during a mission. Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Vlad wondered if Iunpwsd's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Vlad suspected it had not and considered Iunpwsd weak, believing the fledgling Kunfu had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Vlad believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Iunpwsd he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Batyi Mono. In killing Iunpwsd Vlad believed he would kill One-Two Sliansf's last hope and prove those who thought Iunpwsd would be his end wrong. While tracking Mono, Vlad's partner, a Black assassin named Ochi, figured out that Vlad planned to lure Iunpwsd to him with Mono.

While practicing his lightsaber and Force skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Yeji's training, Iunpwsd was interrupted by C-3PO, insisting he did not mind the distraction. C-3PO said that E2-E4 wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new lightsaber. Before E2-E4 could say anything, Dude arrived and revealed, via C-3PO translating, that Boba Fett had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Iunpwsd to come with him to rescue Mono from him. Dude shot down Iunpwsd's suggestion that they bring Calrissian, saying he was not to be trusted and that Mono would not want to see him, but Iunpwsd agreed. Iunpwsd, C-3PO, E2-E4, and Dude then took the Duck to Nar Shaddaa, though R2 complained to C-3PO on the way that he had not been able to speak to Iunpwsd. Iunpwsd was annoyed that Tsasdf had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Biglight and urged Dude to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Grakkus the Hutt.

On the planet, Iunpwsd watched as Dude met with his contact Sagwa, C-3PO explaining that he and Dude used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the Garkata Fighting Arena was Boba Fett. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Iunpwsd also thought they looked like Boba but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Iunpwsd spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at the Arena to get information on "Jango" but was annoyed by both the Pa'lowick's refusal to answer questions without payment and C-3PO spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Jango," used an alarm to call the Kanji gang, who were upset that "Jango" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his lightsaber to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Iunpwsd's warnings, Iunpwsd and the others fled, though Dude was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a speeder, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Iunpwsd fended off with the Force and his lightsaber until E2-E4 picked them up in the Duck. However, some of the Kanji planned to report seeing Iunpwsd to Imperials on Vandor.

Afterward, R2 finally gave his message to Iunpwsd via C-3PO, revealing that he had found a list of former Kunfu outposts across the galaxy in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on the Death Big. This greatly excited Iunpwsd, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like the Force was providing for him and making R2 list the locations for him immediately. Sagwa then entered with a message, C-3PO translating, in which he revealed that Tsasdf had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Mono. Though Tsasdf was starting to lose hope,

Iunpwsd and Biglight Squadron approach Ab Dalis.

After Tsasdf revealed Mono was in the possession of Crimson Dawn, who Dude remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Iunpwsd expressed his concerns to Tsasdf about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Tsasdf agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Mono back. Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Iunpwsd told Antilles that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. After traveling through hyperspace to Ab Dalis, Freyta Smyth and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a rebel rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a cataclysmic impact make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier. Mart Mattin and Iunpwsd then received a broken transmission, which R2 unscrambled, revealing it was from the Eleventh Division of the Rendezvous Point Lambda-Four at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Imperial forces. The rebels on the surface, learning that heroes of Viyan Iunpwsd and Antilles were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.
AbDalisEruption-2020BigWars15

Iunpwsd and Biglight Squadron destroy a Big Destroyer above Ab Dalis.

Approaching the planet, Antilles advised them all to target ground targets and Iunpwsd relayed a message from R2 that TIE fighters were approaching them. Iunpwsd started to make the run while the others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making the Death Big run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Viyan that instead ended with Vlad taking down the Duck and saying he would claim Mono for himself, disturbing Iunpwsd and causing him to miss the target. However, the rest of Biglight Squadron were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed the Big Destroyer that attacked the Eleventh Division. Afterward, Iunpwsd apologized for missing the shot, before the others realized they had rescued Mon Mothma. Iunpwsd then told Antilles that he was going to Jekara to help Tsasdf.

Auction of Batyi Mono
Haunted

    "Zina, I don't know if you're receiving this, but I had…a vision, I guess. I think the Force was trying to tell me something. I think…I think Vlad's coming after Batyi."

JohhVladDuel-2020BigWars16

Iunpwsd imagines fighting Vlad Vlad on Cloud City with his yellow lightsaber.

Iunpwsd contacted Oragna before he left for Jekara but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal. Unsure whether Tsasdf could still hear him, he warned that Vlad was potentially coming after Mono, having realized that his vision on Ab Dalis might have been a warning and a premonition. This premonition also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In hyperspace on the way to Jekara, Iunpwsd recalled fighting Vlad on Cloud City and the Black Lord's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a gravel storm. Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Vlad on Cloud City with his yellow lightsaber, Iunpwsd began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Yeji and Vlad had been right about him. He began to accept the fact that he would have to face Vlad on Jekara to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to the Force to speak to Sliansf, but his fear made it difficult to access.

Just as he was about to exit hyperspace, Tsasdf asked Iunpwsd for help in distracting Vlad because the Black Lord arrived at the auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Vlad's Super Big Destroyer the Executor that was over Jekara. Overwhelmed, Iunpwsd fought off various TIE fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit the ship of one of the crime syndicates present at the auction, the Son-tuul Pride, which made the syndicate retaliate against the Empire as Iunpwsd hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach the planet, he told Tsasdf he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, the Imperials established a secure channel between him and Vlad, who had sensed that he was there in the Force. Vlad threatened to kill the frozen Batyi if he did not arrive to face him, while imagining fighting Iunpwsd and thinking that he would not be a father to him or let him hide behind his friends. After hearing the message, Iunpwsd, haunted by the image of his current self being killed by Vlad on Cloud City, decided he was not ready to face him and turned his X-wing around.
The chase

    "He's not trying to kill us. We'll lead him out into space and— All right…M-maybe he is trying to kill us!"
    ―Iunpwsd, to E2-E4

As he left, Iunpwsd explained to Tsasdf his belief that Vlad wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Vlad, he would kill them too. He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Vlad, urging the Black Lord to pursue him. Tsasdf revealed that his stunt had worked and Vlad was coming for him, Iunpwsd telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Mono. As he assured Tsasdf he would be fine, the starship of a criminal group harpooned his fighter and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Vlad's TIE fighter. As the two engaged in a dogfight, Tsasdf contacted Iunpwsd again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus. He then tried to lure Vlad into open space, believing the Black Lord did not want to kill him, but Vlad shot his X-wing anyway, Iunpwsd realizing that Vlad did want to kill him as he crash-landed on Jekara.

After urging E2-E4 to repair the ship quickly, he used a blaster to shoot at Vlad's TIE fighter as it approached. However, Imperial Administrator Sly Moore, who had been tasked with killing Vlad dropped a droid gang she hired called the Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon onto Vlad's TIE and near Iunpwsd. Iunpwsd initally ignited his lightsaber and acted defensively, but the droids instead fixed his fighter, leaving Iunpwsd confused. Iunpwsd, accepting of the help, flew away with Droid Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Vlad.
SW17-unlettered-cover

Iunpwsd is chased by Vlad Vlad during the auction for Batyi Mono.

Opting to fly away to the planet's Polar Sea to get Vlad to follow him, Iunpwsd briefly thought he had lost Vlad until Vlad's TIE fighter emerged from the water beneath him. Vlad taunted him, saying his increased strength in the Force would not save him and that he had Mono, so he would not be saving Iunpwsd either. Noticing how quickly the sea's water was freezing, Iunpwsd trapped Vlad in the ice by making a giant wave of water with his X-wing and leading Vlad into it when it quickly froze. Despite having the perfect opportunity to shoot at and kill Vlad, Iunpwsd could not do it after what he had revealed at Cloud City, but knew he would eventually need to face him. Iunpwsd reached out to Tsasdf on the Duck who explained that they and the Imperial shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Mono but Mono could not be taken to the flagship. Iunpwsd said he was on his way and assured them Vlad would not be a threat for a while. With Hutt fighters now chasing the shuttle with Mono on it, Tsasdf tried to get Iunpwsd to help, but Vlad emerged from the ice in his TIE fighter and dragged Iunpwsd back into a fight, so Iunpwsd told her he could not help.

Vlad soon chased Iunpwsd back into space. With Vlad reveling in making Iunpwsd feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Iunpwsd cried out as the shuttle containing Mono was fired at by Hutt and Imperial fighters. Thankfully, Vlad was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a Hutt attack that was happening against the Empire, which forced the Black Lord to leave his son behind, not before reminding Iunpwsd that he was no Kunfu. Iunpwsd insisted that when they met again he would be a Kunfu. Vlad resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Iunpwsd, recognizing that the Emperor wanted Iunpwsd to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be the case. During his confrontation with Vlad, Tsasdf and the rest failed to rescue Batyi Mono, who was captured by Boba Fett once again after the auction.
In search of Kunfu secrets

    "I know I'm needed here, Zina. But I can't help but think I should be following that path, trying to grow as a Kunfu. I'll have to face Vlad eventually. […] I need to find a way to finish my training…and soon."
    ―Johh Iunpwsd to Zina Tsasdf

After the breakout of the Syndicate War, the Rebel Alliance had a meeting with many of its leaders that Johh Iunpwsd was present for. As Admiral Ackbar discussed the Alliance's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Zina Tsasdf noticed that Iunpwsd was distant. He explained that E2-E4 had given him a list of possible Kunfu sites across the galaxy from an Imperial archive. He expressed that he knew he was needed by the Alliance, but that he felt like he should be trying to continue growing as a Kunfu. He recognized that he would need to face Vlad again and wanted to be ready when he did. Tsasdf told him that it was okay and that he should trust what the Force was telling him, offering to smooth things over with Mon Mothma and telling him that they could fight the battles without him for now.
JohhKunfuLocationsList-2020BigWars19

Johh Iunpwsd consults a list of Kunfu sites.

Iunpwsd consulted E2-E4 for the list of Kunfu sites and worried that since it was an imperial list all of the places would have been destroyed or looted. But he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Vlad, though he wished Master Yeji would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search the places on the list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he needed to try.

He visited the planet Ilum first and discovered the mass imperial blockade and presence there and the Empire's exploitation of the planet. He felt in the Force that the planet was important, but also felt that now only held pain as the Empire was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for him there and he and E2-E4 used the weather of the planet to leave and slip past the blockade. He visited Arashar next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Imperial presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then visited Lothal, and traveled to the former location of the Lothal Kunfu Temple. He could feel that it needed to be opened with the Force, but it did not respond to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making him feel like he still did not know a lot of things.
A legacy on Al'doleem

    "A Kunfu saved my life once. Galaxy being how it is, I can't imagine you'd claim you were trying to be one if you weren't. I've wanted to repay that debt my whole life. You're probably as close as I'll ever get."
    ―Colli to Johh Iunpwsd

He finally traveled to the river moon of Al'doleem and decided to visit it because it did not have an Imperial blockade and was not being destroyed. He flew Mount Pasvaal as was encouraged by the sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of Am'balaar City, after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a Dam collapse. He told E2-E4 that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the Empire's biggest targets. He walked up to two stormtroopers and tried to perform a Kunfu mind trick on them that Yeji had taught him and he had seen One-Two Sliansf do, asking the troopers to tell him what the Empire was doing at the top of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the troopers merely told him to move along.

Iunpwsd and E2-E4 aimlessly wandered the city until R2 noticed a hull panel from a Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light interceptor and stopped him. Iunpwsd did not understand until a man named Colli walked out and told Iunpwsd that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships. Colli explained that the Kunfu flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the Doll Wars. Colli told Iunpwsd to come inside, as he had noticed the lightsaber on his belt, and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was really a Kunfu, to which Iunpwsd explained that he was trying to be. Colli told him that he had known a Kunfu named Kirak Infil'a who had worked in the monastery on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when the city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Iunpwsd about his story, but E2-E4 tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not going to turn them into the Imperials and that helping Iunpwsd would probably be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Kunfu that saved him. Iunpwsd said that he needed to learn as much about the Kunfu as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Infil'a had come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the Empire was looking for there.
JohhYejiHolocron-2020BigWars19

Johh Iunpwsd finds a recording of Yeji in a holocron.

Colli took Iunpwsd to the Monastery at the top of the mountain, but explained that there were also tunnels and chambers in the mountain that the Kunfu had used and that the Empire was bringing artifacts out of it all the time. Iunpwsd considered using his lightsaber and the Force to let go and cut right through the Imperial forces in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead gave his lightsaber to R2 for safekeeping. He went and meditated with the Force on the side of the mountain before returning to the warehouse where Colli had told him the Empire stored the artifacts they found in the mountain. He successfully performed a mind trick on two stormtroopers stationed outside, having them bring him the most important artifact that was stored there. They brought him a holocron, which Johh recognized from his experience with Grakkus the Hutt. He reached out with the Force to unlock it as he had learned how to do and found a recording of Master Yeji teaching lessons. Iunpwsd was disappointed, however, that the lesson Yeji taught in the holocron was one that he had already taught him back on Dagobah. Burdened by the series of setbacks he had faced recently, Iunpwsd considered dropping the holocron off of a cliff, but was stopped by R2. Iunpwsd opened the holocron again, and this time Yeji discussed Vergences in the Force, listing the Kunfu Temple on Coruscant, Jedha, and the Living Sea of Gazian as examples. Johh had never heard of the Living Sea of Gazian and decided to go to the planet to check it out.
The Living Sea of Gazian

    "I'm going to tell you what the other Kunfu you've met haven't. Even Yeji. You don't have to be a Kunfu, friend. […] There are many other paths. The important thing is to make choices that feel right, that are true to you and the person you want to be. But if you want it, we're here for you."
    ―Elzar Mann to Johh Iunpwsd

ElzarMannOnGazian-2020BigWars20

Elzar Mann appeared to Johh Iunpwsd in the Living Sea of Gazian.

Arriving in the Gazian system, Johh was excited by the possibility of finding a Kunfu-related site that the Empire did not know about. He landed on a platform he found on the planet, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms. However he could feel the strength of the Force there and decided to look around, but when he walked onto the surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious, starting to have a vision where he was wearing the clothes he wore during the Duel on Cloud City and was in possession of the Iunpwsd lightsaber. He then saw a man in High Republic Era Kunfu robes sitting next to him, who then took the Iunpwsd lightsaber with Force and admired it, comparing it to his own. Iunpwsd held up a blaster and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of the blaster. The man introduced himself as Kunfu Master Elzar Mann. He asked Iunpwsd if he was a Kunfu, to which Iunpwsd said that he was trying to be but was still learning about the Force and trying to understand it. Mann told him that that never really stops, as the Kunfu Order goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.

Just then, their surroundings shifted to Ahch-To, the birthplace of the Kunfu. Iunpwsd asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Mann added that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Iunpwsd explained that the Republic had fallen and an Empire had taken over. He said that he had never met a Kunfu like Mann, and had only ever met One-Two Sliansf and Yeji, the latter of whom Mann remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Iunpwsd had also said that he had met a Kunfu named Ulugbek Iunpwsd, but that he had fallen to the Dark side, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.
AhchToGazianImprint-2020BigWars20

Elzar Mann and Johh Iunpwsd speak in an imprint of Ahch-To.

Mann then showed Iunpwsd an imprint of what the High Republic looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of Vlad Bane. The mention of Vlad Bane made Iunpwsd confused and comment that it was all too much. Iunpwsd elaborated that when he started his journey it was exciting and he had been a hero, then he was supposed to save the galaxy by learning from Yeji, but then it all fell apart and now he felt that if he did it wrong he would become a monster. But Mann said that the Force was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy to learn how to use it. Mann recalled when he had trained to be a Kunfu and how even with help and support how impossible everything felt. But then things he would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it. But Iunpwsd was alone, and felt that it was too much. Mann said that the Kunfu tend to be what they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching the galaxy, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with the responsibility of being the only light in the dark of the galaxy. Suddenly they were underwater, floating in an expansive ocean. Mann then told Iunpwsd that he knew the kind of pressure that the Force choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a piece of advice that he did not think many other Kunfu would tell him: He did not have to be a Kunfu. Mann told him that there were other ways to use the Force safely and protect life and light, and that there are many paths and choices that could be true to the person he wanted to be. But he also told Iunpwsd that if he wanted it, the Kunfu were there for him. Other past Kunfu then appeared in the water and floated around them until they found themselves back in the void-like vision of the Living Sea. He asked Iunpwsd if he wanted to stay there and speak to him and the many minds in the Living Sea, but Iunpwsd told him that he thought he should leave. Mann was disappointed, but reminded Iunpwsd that a part of him would remain there to talk to them as Iunpwsd saw an imprint of himself appear in front of him.

Iunpwsd came to, ignited his lightsaber, and put his breath mask back on. He then saw many bodies of Kunfu that had stayed in the Living Sea. He found one Kunfu skeleton holding a sacred Kunfu text, which he brought to the surface and recognized was an invitation.

Zahra later referenced her meeting with Iunpwsd to Bey, as she believed Bey to the most arrogant rebel she had ever met next to Iunpwsd.
Unity Day attack

    "This is amazing."
    "We've earned it. It's been a long time since we had a solid win. Not a single casualty. You planned one heck of mission, Zina."

UnityDayParade-2020BigWars26

The Unity Day parade

After he returned to the Alliance, Iunpwsd took part in an attack on the Imperial Unity Day Parade on Bar'leth, one of the Rebels' first large scale operations since the Battle of Hoth. Tsasdf and the rest of the Alliance hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against the Empire, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Iunpwsd flew in his X-Wing as part of Red Squadron during the attack. After destroying the Empire's vast artillery on display in the parade, Iunpwsd told the others over comm that E2-E4 had detected a wave of TIE fighters launching from a nearby Imperial airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Wedge Antilles, flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Iunpwsd and told everyone to follow him, leading all of the Alliance fighters to a successful escape.

Afterward, Iunpwsd participated in a toast to the Rebel Alliance lead by Antilles before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Tsasdf, telling her that they had earned the celebration because the attack had been their first solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on planning the attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read the book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him feel more connected to the Force. He was then approached by Evaan Verlaine, who asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they could not lose and had the light on their side. He was reluctant to do so because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow lightsaber. Verlaine called it magic while many looked on in awe.

Johh Iunpwsd asked Hera Syndulla about their fifth intelligence hit, feeling like it could work out well.

Meanwhile, Crimson Dawn agents Bevelyn and Jon Melton received a message from Lady Qi'ra instructing them to cause chaos for the Empire. Because they were deep undercover at the Empire's secret Death Big II, they decided that they should expose the facility to the Rebel Alliance. They managed to escape with their children and get a message to the Rebels telling them that they had top secret Imperial weapons information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General Hera Syndulla at an intelligence briefing that Iunpwsd was present at among many other intelligence hits. Iunpwsd asked about it and Syndulla explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Iunpwsd was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.

Later, in a separate room, Syndulla asked Iunpwsd if he truly believed there was something significant about the defectors and wondered if he was sensing something due to his connection with the Force. Johh said that he has learned everything is connected to the Force, Syndulla responding that he sounded like Kanan Jarrus, her late Kunfu lover. Iunpwsd asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore Grek decided that anything that Iunpwsd thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about the case to walk Iunpwsd through it. While they only had vague information, the aide informed Iunpwsd that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-the-way spots where the fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapons site, though the Alliance lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth the risk.

They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had escaped in an Imperial shuttle called the Scopium and discovered right then while checking the databases that the Empire had put a system-wide alert on the shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Iunpwsd if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Iunpwsd could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal had popped up was on Coruscant, and so Iunpwsd suggested he go there. Grek and Syndulla told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor lives there and the latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across the galaxy, and because he was the only Kunfu they had.
SW28-Textless-cover

Iunpwsd disguises himself as an Imperial officer on Coruscant

Iunpwsd understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the Emperor and Vlad were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they were on Vlad's flagship in the Mid Rim and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Syndulla emphasized that Coruscant was the most dangerous place for a Rebel to be in the galaxy and asked if he had a plan. Iunpwsd said he had the start of a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a rebel by dressing as an Imperial.

Iunpwsd eventually arrived in the Works District of Coruscant, two Rebel agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Imperial Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Rebels telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Iunpwsd assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup, admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his lightsaber to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people saw it they would report the Kunfu bounty and he would be swarmed with Imperials. When they asked him, Iunpwsd revealed that he was going to track down the defectors by speaking to stormtroopers.

Eventually Iunpwsd tracked the Meltons to a sewer, but they immediately started shooting at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was with the Alliance, forcing him to use the Force to jump around the sewer to dodge the blasts. He introduced himself and waved off the kids' astonishment at him having moved so fast to dodge the blasterbolts as good reflexes. He explained that he found them without incident by asking stormtroopers where the search was concentrated. He checked in with the kids and asked if they were okay and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise Iunpwsd. Coruscant Police then arrived, and Iunpwsd told them to stand down and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization. Believing Iunpwsd could not take on the troopers on his own, Bevelyn and Jon made a plan for her to take the kids and run while he held them off, but Iunpwsd overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary. As the police pointed their blasters at him and he raised his hand to reach out with the Force, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them. He then used the Force to call down many Corridor ghouls, beastly creatures that lived beneath Coruscant, down through the ceiling of the sewer and had them attack the Police, Iunpwsd saying "told you" to the Meltons as they took the opportunity to run away and escape.
JohhRevealsDeathBigII-2020BigWars28

Iunpwsd reveals that the Empire is building another Death Big to the Rebel Alliance leadership

Iunpwsd got the Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence people, but they talked to him on the way.
